### Exact-object chronology, taxonomy and proof limits
- SAMR 20265082-Z-469 current `正在起草` means TC28 non-API collaboration guidance-document registration, not published standard.
- SAMR 20265083-Z-469 current `正在起草` identifies distinct TC28 context-sharing guidance-document registration.
- Same-day matching TC28 status text is not a normative mapping between their clauses.
- SAMR 20256913-T-907 current `正在批准` is a recommended national standard PLAN, not issued GB/T text.
- Historical `正在审查` versus `正在批准` 20256913 displays have unresolved state transition sequence/date, preserved as UNVERIFIED.
- SAMR 20262581-Z-907 current `正在起草` is a separate TC609 guidance-document registration.
- ITU-T F.748.93 approved 2026-08-29, `In force (prepublished)` does not certify agent conformance.
- Related standard/project links can show discovery relevance, not official GB/Z–MCP/A2A normative crosswalk.
- Publication, effective date, implementation and certified conformance each require separate primary evidence.
- C-W31, C-W79 and C-W80 remain OPEN, not closed by same-page source rechecks.
- W41 H41-1/H41-2/H41-3 unchanged and OPEN within native C1–C8 outcome.
- Same SAMR/ITU publisher lineage cannot be promoted into an independent source simply by rechecking.
- One new native dated Daily is retained; A2 owner adds zero producer Daily, event, source-registry, watcher, history or ledger increments.
- Current October monthly top checkpoint 10/10 and ten real Dailies, 10/11–31 future and NOT_DUE.
- No live deployment, standards cross-implementation, certification, benchmark or external runtime executed by A2.
- October OPEN; September historical missing window and all A1/A2 earlier observations preserved.
